        //------------ read write connect wallet ------------------------------------

        function switchReadWriteOtherWallet(type) {
            document.getElementById("readWriteWalletList").classList.add("d-none");
            document.getElementById("otherReadWriteWalletList").classList.remove("d-none");
            document.getElementById("readWriteBackButton").classList.remove("d-none");
            document.getElementById("readWriteBackButtonInfo").classList.remove("d-none");
            document.getElementById("exampleModalLabel").innerText = "Other Wallets";

            const searchInput = document.getElementById("otherReadWriteWalletSearch");
            searchInput.value = "";

            renderOtherReadWriteWalletList(type);
            filterOtherReadWriteWalletList();
            searchInput.focus();
        }

        function showReadWriteWalletList() {
            document.getElementById("readWriteWalletList").classList.remove("d-none");
            document.getElementById("otherReadWriteWalletList").classList.add("d-none");
            document.getElementById("readWriteBackButton").classList.add("d-none");
            document.getElementById("readWriteBackButtonInfo").classList.add("d-none");
            document.getElementById("exampleModalLabel").innerText = "Connect a Wallet";

            document.getElementById("otherReadWriteWalletSearch").value = "";
            document.getElementById("otherReadWriteWalletNoSearchResult").classList.add("d-none");
        }

        function renderOtherReadWriteWalletList(type) {
            const container = document.getElementById("otherReadWriteWalletContainer");
            const noSearchResult = document.getElementById("otherReadWriteWalletNoSearchResult");

            container.innerHTML = "";
            noSearchResult.classList.add("d-none");

            //list wallet to filter out
            const excludedWallets = ["Rabby Wallet", "Coinbase Wallet", "MetaMask"];

            const uniqueProviders = [...new Map(providers.map(p => [p.info.name, p])).values()].filter(wallet => !excludedWallets.includes(wallet.info.name));

            if (uniqueProviders.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = '<div class="text-muted text-center py-3">No available wallet</div>';
                return;
            }

            uniqueProviders.forEach(wallet => {
                container.innerHTML += `
                <button type="button" data-wallet-name="${filterXSS(wallet.info.name.toLowerCase())}" onclick="connectOther('${filterXSS(type)}','${filterXSS(wallet.info.name)}')" class="btn btn-lg btn-wallet d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center w-100 p-3 mb-2">
                    <span class="text-dark">${filterXSS(wallet.info.name)}</span>
                    <img width="25" src="${filterXSS(wallet.info.icon)}" alt="${filterXSS(wallet.info.name)}">
                </button>
            `;
            });
        }


        function filterOtherReadWriteWalletList() {
            const searchInput = document.getElementById("otherReadWriteWalletSearch");
            const container = document.getElementById("otherReadWriteWalletContainer");
            const noSearchResult = document.getElementById("otherReadWriteWalletNoSearchResult");

            const keyword = searchInput.value.trim().toLowerCase();
            const buttons = container.querySelectorAll("button");

            let visibleCount = 0;

            buttons.forEach(btn => {
                const text = btn.textContent.toLowerCase();
                const walletName = (btn.getAttribute("data-wallet-name") || "").toLowerCase();

                const isMatch = text.includes(keyword) || walletName.includes(keyword);

                if (isMatch) {
                    btn.classList.remove("d-none");
                    btn.classList.add("d-flex");
                    visibleCount++;
                } else {
                    btn.classList.remove("d-flex");
                    btn.classList.add("d-none");
                }
            });

            const hasButtons = buttons.length > 0;
            noSearchResult.classList.toggle("d-none", !(hasButtons && visibleCount === 0));
        }

        document.getElementById("otherReadWriteWalletSearch").addEventListener("input", filterOtherReadWriteWalletList);



        const walletModal = document.getElementById('WalletModal');

        walletModal.addEventListener('hidden.bs.modal', function () {
            showReadWriteWalletList();
        });


        //----------------------------------------------

        function resetPath() {
            window.location.href = "#auditReportId";
            var baseUrl = window.location.href.split('#')[0];
            history.replaceState("", document.title, baseUrl + '#' + 'code');
        }

        function walletConnect_reown(id) {
            var elem = document.getElementById(id);
            elem.contentWindow.connectWeb3('walletConnect_reown');
            elem.style.height = '750px';
        }

        function connectRabby(id) {
            var elem = document.getElementById(id);
            elem.contentWindow.connectWeb3('rabby');
            elem.style.height = '750px';
        }

        function connectOther(id, name) {
            var elem = document.getElementById(id);
            elem.contentWindow.connectWeb3(name, providers);
            elem.style.height = '750px';
        }

        function removeHeight() {
            var element = document.getElementById('writecontractiframe');
            element.style.removeProperty("min-height");
        }

Slot C: money and score bookkeeping.\\n    struct Cat {\\n        \/\/ slot A\\n        uint40 lastFed; \/\/ 0 = fresh: refs are implied by the mint time and WELCOME\\n        uint40 lastWashed;\\n        uint40 lastPlayed;\\n        uint40 energyRef; \/\/ see _energyCalc(): the moment energy is\/was 100 and the cat awake\\n        uint40 poopAt; \/\/ when the current poop appears\/appeared; 0 = none scheduled\\n        uint40 deadAt; \/\/ 0 = not recorded dead\\n        uint16 streak;\\n        \/\/ slot B\\n        uint24 feeds;\\n        uint24 washes;\\n        uint24 plays;\\n        uint24 naps;\\n        uint24 cleanups;\\n        uint24 pets;\\n        uint16 names;\\n        uint16 deaths;\\n        uint16 revives;\\n        uint16 careScore; \/\/ 0..10000 as of scoreAt\\n        uint24 lastCareDay;\\n        uint24 lastPetDay;\\n        \/\/ slot C\\n        uint128 monPaid;\\n        uint40 bornAt; \/\/ when the clock started\\n        uint40 scoreAt; \/\/ last careScore update\\n        uint40 scoreFrom; \/\/ start of the score history (clock start, or the last revive)\\n    }\\n\\n    mapping(uint256 =\u003e Cat) private _cats;\\n    mapping(uint256 =\u003e string) private _names;\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @dev Crown list. Entry: (id \u003c\u003c 32) | (score \u003c\u003c 16) | streak, 64 bits, four per storage slot so a\\n    \/\/\/      full rescan touches CROWNS \/ 4 slots (cold storage is dear on Monad). Index+1 per id in _crownIndex.\\n    uint256 private constant CROWN_SLOTS = CROWNS \/ 4;\\n    uint256 private constant ENTRY_MASK = 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFF;\\n    uint256 private constant KEY_MASK = 0xFFFFFFFF;\\n    uint256[CROWN_SLOTS] private _crownSlots;\\n    uint256 private _crownLen;\\n    mapping(uint256 =\u003e uint256) private _crownIndex;\\n    uint256 private _crownMin; \/\/ index of the entry with the lowest (score, streak)\\n\\n    uint256 public pendingBurnMon;\\n    uint128 public treasuryOwed;\\n    uint128 public teamOwed;\\n    uint256 public totalEmoBurned;\\n    uint256 public totalMonBurned;\\n    uint256 private _lock = 1;\\n\\n    \/\/ ---------------------------------------------------------------- setup\\n    struct Params {\\n        address minter;\\n string siteURI;\\n    }\\n\\n    constructor(Params memory p) {\\n        if (\\n            p.minter == address(0) || p.treasury == address(0) || p.team == address(0) || p.emo == address(0)\\n                || p.wmon == address(0) || p.router == address(0) || p.lens == address(0) || p.pool == address(0)\\n                || p.art == address(0)\\n        ) revert ZeroAddress();\\n        if (p.burnBps + p.treasuryBps + p.teamBps != BPS || p.reviveBurnBps \u003e BPS) revert BadSplit();\\n        if (p.maxImpactBps == 0 || p.maxImpactBps \u003e BPS) revert BadGuard();\\n        MINTER = p.minter;\\n        MAX_SUPPLY = p.maxSupply;\\n        WELCOME = p.welcome;\\n        TREASURY = p.treasury;\\n        TEAM = p.team;\\n        BURN_BPS = p.burnBps;\\n        TREASURY_BPS = p.treasuryBps;\\n        TEAM_BPS = p.teamBps;\\n        REVIVE_BURN_BPS = p.reviveBurnBps;\\n        EMO = p.emo;\\n        WMON = p.wmon;\\n        ROUTER = INadRouter(p.router);\\n        LENS =        if (_lock != 1) revert Reentrancy();\\n        _lock = 2;\\n        _;\\n        _lock = 1;\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/ ================================================================ minting (airdrop)\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @notice Mint one cat to each address. Minter only, capped by MAX_SUPPLY.\\n    function mint(address[] calldata to) external {\\n        if (msg.sender != MINTER) revert NotMinter();\\n        uint256 id = totalSupply;\\n        if (to.length == 0 || id + to.length \u003e MAX_SUPPLY) revert SoldOut();\\n        _batches.push(((id + 1) \u003c\u003c 48) | block.timestamp);\\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i \u003c to.length; i++) {\\n            _mint(to[i], ++id);\\n        }\\n        totalSupply = id;\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @notice Mint \`count\` cats to one address. Minter only, capped by MAX_SUPPLY.\\n    function mintMany(address to, uint256 count) external {\\n        if (msg.sender != MINTER) revert NotMinter();\\n        uint256 id = totalSupply;\\n        if (count == 0 || id + count \u003e MAX_SUPPLY) revert SoldOut();\\n        _batches.push(((id + 1) \u003c\u003c 48) | block.timestamp);\\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i \u003c count; i++) {\\n            _mint(to, ++id);\\n        }\\n        totalSupply = id;\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/ ================================================================ paid care\\n\\n    function feed(uint256 id) external payable {\\n        _paidCare(id, 0);\\n    }\\n\\n    function play(uint256 id) external payable {\\n        _paidCare(id, 1);\\n    }\\n\\n    function wash(uint256 id) external payable {\\n        _paidCare(id, 2);\\n    }\\n\\n    function sleep(uint256 id) external payable {\\n        _paidCare(id, 3);\\n    }\\n\\n    function clean(uint256 id) external payable {\\n        _paidCare(id, 4);\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @notice Batch: one action per id, value = ids.length x PRICE. Actions: 0 feed, 1 play, 2 wash, 3 sleep, 4 clean.\\n    function care(uint256[] calldata ids, uint8[] calldata actions) external payable {\\n        if (ids.length != actions.length) revert LengthMismatch();\\n        if (ids.length == 0 || ids.length \u003e MAX_BATCH) revert BadCount();\\n        uint256 required = ids.length * PRICE;\\n        if (msg.value != required) revert WrongValue(required, msg.value);\\n        _split(required);\\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i \u003c ids.length; i++) {\\n            _care(ids[i], actions[i], PRICE);\\n        }\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @notice Wake a sleeping cat. Free.\\n    function wake(uint256 id) external {\\n        Cat storage c = _cats[id];\\n        if (_ownerOf[id] != msg.sender) revert NotOwner();\\n        _sync(id, c);\\n        if (!_alive(id, c)) revert NotAlive();\\n        _materialize(id, c);\\n        (, bool asleep,) = _energyCalc(c.energyRef, block.timestamp);\\n        if (!asleep) revert Awake();\\n        _wake(c, block.timestamp);\\n        emit Care(id, 5, msg.sender, 0, block.timestamp);\\n        emit MetadataUpdate(id);\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @notice Pet the cat, up to MAX_PETS times in one call. Gas only. The first pet each UTC day gives +PET_FUN fun.\\n    function pet(uint256 id, uint256 count) external {\\n        if (count == 0 || count \u003e MAX_PETS) revert BadCount();\\n        Cat storage c = _cats[id];\\n        if (_ownerOf[id] != msg.sender) revert NotOwner();\\n        _sync(id, c);\\n        if (!_alive(id, c)) revert NotAlive();\\n        \/\/ a pet never starts the clock, but once the welcome is over the cat is live and the bonus applies\\n        if (c.lastFed == 0 \u0026\u0026 block.timestamp \u003e= _mintedAt(id) + WELCOME) _materialize(id, c);\\n        c.pets += uint24(count);\\n        uint24 today = uint24(block.timestamp \/ 1 days);\\n        bool bonus;\\n        if (c.lastPetDay != today) {\\n            c.lastPetDay = today;\\n            bonus = true;\\n            if (c.lastFed != 0) {\\n                uint256 lp = uint256(c.lastPlayed) + (PET_FUN * DRAIN) \/ 100;\\n                c.lastPlayed = uint40(lp \u003e block.timestamp ? block.timestamp : lp);\\n            }\\n        }\\n        emit Petted(id, msg.sender, count, bonus);\\n        emit MetadataUpdate(id);\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @notice Name or rename the cat. 1..32 bytes. NAME_PRICE.\\n    function setName(uint256 id, string calldata newName) external payable {\\n        if (msg.value != NAME_PRICE) revert WrongValue(NAME_PRICE, msg.value);\\n        uint256 len = bytes(newName).length;\\n        if (len == 0 || len \u003e NAME_BYTES) revert BadName();\\n        Cat storage c = _cats[id];\\n        if (_ownerOf[id] != msg.sender) revert NotOwner();\\n        _sync(id, c);\\n        if (!_alive(id, c)) revert NotAlive();\\n        _split(NAME_PRICE);\\n        c.names += 1;\\n        c.monPaid += uint128(NAME_PRICE);\\n        _names[id] = newName;\\n        emit Named(id, newName);\\n        emit Care(id, 6, msg.sender, NAME_PRICE, block.timestamp);\\n        emit MetadataUpdate(id);\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @notice Bring a dead cat back with every meter at REVIVE_METER. REVIVE_PRICE.\\n    function revive(uint256 id) external payable {\\n        if (msg.value != REVIVE_PRICE) revert WrongValue(REVIVE_PRICE, msg.value);\\n        if (_ownerOf[id] != msg.sender) revert NotOwner();\\n        _splitRevive();\\n        _revive(id);\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @notice Record anything that happened to the cat by itself (death), refresh its rank and metadata. Anyone.\\n    function poke(uint256 id) external {\\n        if (_ownerOf[id] == address(0)) revert InvalidToken();\\n        Cat storage c = _cats[id];\\n        _sync(id, c);\\n        if (c.lastFed != 0 \u0026\u0026 c.deadAt == 0) _rank(id, _scoreCalc(id, c, block.timestamp), c.streak);\\n        emit MetadataUpdate(id);\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/ ================================================================ direct transfer protocol\\n\\n    receive() external payable nonReentrant {\\n        uint256 v = msg.value;\\n        if (v == 0 || v % TENTH != 0) revert BadAmount();\\n        uint256 n = v \/ 1 ether;\\n        uint256 d = (v % 1 ether) \/ TENTH;\\n        if (n == 0 || d \u003e 4) revert BadAmount();\\n\\n        if (n == 1000 \u0026\u0026 d == 0) {\\n            uint256 deadId = _longestDead(msg.sender);\\n            if (deadId != 0) {\\n                _splitRevive();\\n                _revive(deadId);\\n                return;\\n            }\\n        }\\n        if (n \u003e MAX_BATCH) revert BadAmount();\\n\\n        uint256[] memory ids = _select(msg.sender, uint8(d), n);\\n        _split(n * 1 ether);\\n        treasuryOwed += uint128(d * TENTH);\\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i \u003c n; i++) {\\n            _care(ids[i], uint8(d), PRICE);\\n        }\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/ ================================================================ permissionless plumbing\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @notice Push accumulated burn MON through nad.fun, up to maxMon, under the impact guard. \`minEmoOut\`\\n    \/\/\/         is an optional floor from the caller (a quote taken in an earlier block); the swap uses the\\n    \/\/\/         higher of it and the contract\u0027s own guard, and a fill below it is queued, never lost.\\n    function crankBurn(uint256 maxMon, uint256 minEmoOut) external nonReentrant {\\n        uint256 amount = pendingBurnMon \u003c maxMon ? pendingBurnMon : maxMon;\\n        if (amount == 0) revert NothingToDo();\\n        pendingBurnMon -= amount;\\n        _burn(amount, minEmoOut);\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @notice MON that reached the contract outside the protocol (forced sends) joins the burn queue. Anyone.\\n    function skim() external nonReentrant {\\n        uint256 tracked = pendingBurnMon + treasuryOwed + teamOwed;\\n        uint256 excess = address(this).balance - tracked;\\n        if (excess == 0) revert NothingToDo();\\n        pendingBurnMon += excess;\\n        emit Skimmed(excess);\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @notice Pay the treasury and the team what they are owed. Anyone. A share whose receiver refuses stays owed.\\n    function sweep() external nonReentrant {\\n        uint256 t = treasuryOwed;\\n        uint256 m = teamOwed;\\n        if (t == 0 \u0026\u0026 m == 0) revert NothingToDo();\\n        uint256 paidT;\\n        uint256 paidM;\\n        if (t \u003e 0) {\\n            treasuryOwed = 0;\\n            (bool ok,) = TREASURY.call{value: t}(\\\"\\\");\\n            if (ok) paidT = t;\\n            else treasuryOwed = uint128(t);\\n        }\\n        if (m \u003e 0) {\\n            teamOwed = 0;\\n            (bool ok,) = TEAM.call{value: m}(\\\"\\\");\\n            if (ok) paidM = m;\\n            else teamOwed = uint128(m);\\n        }\\n        if (paidT == 0 \u0026\u0026 paidM == 0) revert NothingToDo();\\n        emit Swept(paidT, paidM);\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/ ================================================================ views\\n\\n    struct View {\\n        uint256 id;\\n        address owner;\\n        string name;\\n        bool started;    function approve(address spender, uint256 id) external {\\n        address owner = ownerOf(id);\\n        if (msg.sender != owner \u0026\u0026 !isApprovedForAll[owner][msg.sender]) revert Unauthorized();\\n        getApproved[id] = spender;\\n        emit Approval(owner, spender, id);\\n    }\\n\\n    function setApprovalForAll(address operator, bool approved) external {\\n        isApprovedForAll[msg.sender][operator] = approved;\\n        emit ApprovalForAll(msg.sender, operator, approved);\\n    }\\n\\n    function transferFrom(address from, address to, uint256 id) public {\\n        if (from != _ownerOf[id]) revert NotOwner();\\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\\n        if (msg.sender != from \u0026\u0026 !isApprovedForAll[from][msg.sender] \u0026\u0026 msg.sender != getApproved[id]) {\\n            revert Unauthorized();\\n        }\\n        _removeOwned(from, id);\\n        _addOwned(to, id);\\n        _ownerOf[id] = to;\\n        delete getApproved[id];\\n        emit Transfer(from, to, id);\\n    }\\n\\n    function safeTransferFrom(address from, address to, uint256 id) external {\\n        transferFrom(from, to, id);\\n        _checkReceiver(from, to, id, \\\"\\\");\\n    }\\n\\n    function safeTransferFrom(address from, address to, uint256 id, bytes calldata data) external {\\n        transferFrom(from, to, id);\\n        _checkReceiver(from, to, id, data);\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/ ================================================================ internals: care\\n\\n    function _paidCare(uint256 id, uint8 action) private {\\n        if (msg.value != PRICE) revert WrongValue(PRICE, msg.value);\\n        _split(PRICE);\\n        _care(id, action, PRICE);\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @dev Apply one paid action. The caller has already taken the payment into the split.\\n    function _care(uint256 id, uint8 action, uint256 paid) private {\\n        Cat storage c = _cats[id];\\n        if (_ownerOf[id] != msg.sender) revert NotOwner();\\n        _sync(id, c);\\n        if (!_alive(id, c)) revert NotAlive();\\n        uint256 now_ = block.timestamp;\\n        _materialize(id, c);\\n        c.careScore = uint16(_scoreFrom(c, now_));\\n        c.scoreAt = uint40(now_);\\n        if (action == 0) {\\n            c.lastFed = uint40(now_);\\n            c.feeds += 1;\\n            if (!_poop(c.poopAt, now_)) c.poopAt = uint40(now_ + POOP_AFTER);\\n        } else if (action == 1) {\\n            c.lastPlayed = uint40(now_);\\n            c.plays += 1;\\n        } else if (action == 2) {\\n            c.lastWashed = uint40(now_);\\n            c.washes += 1;\\n        } else if (action == 3) {\\n            (uint256 e, bool asleep,) = _energyCalc(c.energyRef, now_);\\n            if (asleep) revert Asleep();\\n            c.energyRef = uint40(now_ + ((100 - e) * REFILL) \/ 100);\\n            c.naps += 1;\\n        } else if (action == 4) {\\n            if (!_poop(c.poopAt, now_)) revert NoPoop();\\n            uint256 cur = _cleanCalc(c.lastWashed, c.poopAt, now_);\\n            uint256 next = cur + CLEANUP_BONUS \u003e 100 ? 100 : cur + CLEANUP_BONUS;\\n            c.poopAt = 0;\\n            c.lastWashed = uint40(now_ - ((100 - next) * DRAIN) \/ 100);\\n            c.cleanups += 1;\\n        } else {\\n            revert BadAction();\\n        }\\n        if (action != 3) {\\n            \/\/ a sleeping cat is woken to be fed, played with, washed or cleaned up after\\n            (, bool asleep,) = _energyCalc(c.energyRef, now_);\\n            if (asleep) _wake(c, now_);\\n        }\\n        _streak(c, now_);\\n        c.monPaid += uint128(paid);\\n        _rank(id, c.careScore, c.streak);\\n        emit Care(id, action, msg.sender, paid, now_);\\n        emit MetadataUpdate(id);\\n    }\\n\\n    function _revive(uint256 id) private {\\n        Cat storage c = _cats[id];\\n        _sync(id, c);\\n        if (_alive(id, c)) revert Alive();\\n        uint256 now_ = block.timestamp;\\n        uint40 ref = uint40(now_ - ((100 - REVIVE_METER) * DRAIN) \/ 100);\\n        c.lastFed = ref;\\n        c.lastWashed = ref;\\n        c.lastPlayed = ref;\\n        c.energyRef = ref;\\n        c.poopAt = 0;\\n        c.deadAt = 0;\\n        c.revives += 1;\\n        c.monPaid += uint128(REVIVE_PRICE);\\n        c.careScore = 0;\\n        c.scoreAt = uint40(now_);\\n        c.scoreFrom = uint40(now_);\\n        _streak(c, now_);\\n        _rank(id, c.careScore, c.streak);\\n        emit Revived(id, msg.sender);\\n        emit Care(id, 7, msg.sender, REVIVE_PRICE, now_);\\n        emit MetadataUpdate(id);\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @dev A fresh cat gets explicit references: now if still in the welcome period, else the moment it ended.\\n    function _materialize(uint256 id, Cat storage c) private {\\n        if (c.lastFed != 0) return;\\n        uint256 start = _mintedAt(id) + WELCOME;\\n        uint40 base = uint40(block.timestamp \u003c start ? block.timestamp : start);\\n        c.lastFed = base;\\n        c.lastWashed = base;\\n        c.lastPlayed = base;\\n        c.energyRef = base;\\n        c.bornAt = base;\\n        c.careScore = 0;\\n        c.scoreAt = base;\\n        c.scoreFrom = base;\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @dev Same as _materialize for a memory copy whose clock started at \`start\`.\\n    function _imply(Cat memory c, uint256 start) private pure {\\n        c.lastFed = uint40(start);\\n        c.lastWashed = uint40(start);\\n        c.lastPlayed = uint40(start);\\n        c.energyRef = uint40(start);\\n        c.bornAt = uint40(start);\\n        c.careScore = 0;\\n        c.scoreAt = uint40(start);\\n        c.scoreFrom = uint40(start);\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @dev Record a death that happened by itself. Idempotent.\\n    function _sync(uint256 id, Cat storage c) private {\\n        if (c.deadAt != 0) return;\\n        uint256 fed = c.lastFed;\\n        if (fed == 0) {\\n            uint256 start = _mintedAt(id) + WELCOME;\\n            if (block.timestamp \u003c start + DEATH_AFTER) return;\\n            _materialize(id, c);\\n            fed = c.lastFed;\\n        }\\n        uint256 dies = fed + DEATH_AFTER;\\n        if (block.timestamp \u003e= dies) {\\n            c.deadAt = uint40(dies);\\n            c.deaths += 1;\\n            c.streak = 0;\\n            _uncrown(id);\\n            emit Died(id, dies);\\n        }\\n    }\\n\\n    function _alive(uint256 id, Cat storage c) private view returns (bool) {\\n        if (c.deadAt != 0) return false;\\n        uint256 fed = c.lastFed;\\n        if (fed == 0) fed = _mintedAt(id) + WELCOME;\\n        return block.timestamp \u003c fed + DEATH_AFTER;\\n    }\\n\\n    function _wake(Cat storage c, uint256 now_) private {\\n        (uint256 e,,) = _energyCalc(c.energyRef, now_);\\n        uint256 elapsed = ((100 - e) * DRAIN) \/ 100;\\n        if (elapsed \u003e= DRAIN) elapsed = DRAIN - 1; \/\/ never wake straight back to sleep\\n        c.energyRef = uint40(now_ - elapsed);\\n    }\\n\\n    function _streak(Cat storage c, uint256 now_) private {\\n        uint24 today = uint24(now_ \/ 1 days);\\n        if (c.lastCareDay == today \u0026\u0026 c.streak != 0) return;\\n        c.streak = c.lastCareDay + 1 == today ? c.streak + 1 : 1;\\n        c.lastCareDay = today;\\n    }\\n\\n    function _split(uint256 amount) private {\\n        uint256 toTreasury = (amount * TREASURY_BPS) \/ BPS;\\n        uint256 toTeam = (amount * TEAM_BPS) \/ BPS;\\n        treasuryOwed += uint128(toTreasury);\\n        teamOwed += uint128(toTeam);\\n        pendingBurnMon += amount - toTreasury - toTeam;\\n    }\\n\\n    function _splitRevive() private {\\n        uint256 toBurn = (REVIVE_PRICE * REVIVE_BURN_BPS) \/ BPS;\\n        pendingBurnMon += toBurn;\\n        teamOwed += uint128(REVIVE_PRICE - toBurn);\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/ ================================================================ internals: score and crown\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @dev Average of the four meters x100 at time t (the cat must be started and alive).\\n    function _avgMem(Cat memory c, uint256 t) private pure returns (uint256) {\\n        (uint256 e,,) = _energyCalc(c.energyRef, t);\\n        return (_meter(c.lastFed, t) + _cleanCalc(c.lastWashed, c.poopAt, t) + _meter(c.lastPlayed, t) + e) * 25;\\n    }\\n\\n    uint256 private constant SCORE_SAMPLES = 8;\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @dev Score as of t for a started, alive cat: the stored score blended with the time-average of the\\n    \/\/\/      meters over [scoreAt, t] (midpoint rule, SCORE_SAMPLES slices; no care happened in between, so\\n    \/\/\/      the meters are a pure function of time). The blend window is the cat\u0027s score history, capped at\\n    \/\/\/      SCORE_WINDOW: a true running average for the first week, an exponential one after.\\n    function _scoreFrom(Cat memory c, uint256 t) private pure returns (uint256) {\\n        uint256 dt = t - c.scoreAt;\\n        if (dt == 0) return c.careScore;\\n        uint256 window = t - c.scoreFrom;\\n        if (window \u003e SCORE_WINDOW) window = SCORE_WINDOW;\\n        uint256 w = dt \u003e window ? window : dt;\\n        uint256 from = t - w; \/\/ only the last \`window\` of the interval can matter\\n        uint256 sum;\\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i \u003c SCORE_SAMPLES; i++) {\\n            sum += _avgMem(c, from + (w * (2 * i + 1)) \/ (2 * SCORE_SAMPLES));\\n        }\\n        uint256 sample = sum \/ SCORE_SAMPLES;\\n        return (uint256(c.careScore) * (window - w) + sample * w) \/ window;\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @dev Live score for any cat: 0 while fresh, 0 when dead.\\n    function _scoreCalc(uint256 id, Cat storage cs, uint256 t) private view returns (uint256) {\\n        Cat memory c = cs;\\n        if (c.lastFed == 0) {\\n            uint256 start = _mintedAt(id) + WELCOME;\\n            if (t \u003c start) return 0;\\n            _imply(c, start);\\n        }\\n        if (c.deadAt != 0 || t \u003e= uint256(c.lastFed) + DEATH_AFTER) return 0;\\n        return _scoreFrom(c, t);\\n    }\\n\\n    function _key(uint256 score, uint256 streak) private pure returns (uint256) {\\n        return (score \u003c\u003c 16) | streak;\\n    }\\n\\n    function _entry(uint256 i) private view returns (uint256) {\\n        return (_crownSlots[i \u003e\u003e 2] \u003e\u003e ((i \u0026 3) \u003c\u003c 6)) \u0026 ENTRY_MASK;\\n    }\\n\\n    function _setEntry(uint256 i, uint256 e) private {\\n        uint256 shift = (i \u0026 3) \u003c\u003c 6;\\n        uint256 slot = _crownSlots[i \u003e\u003e 2];\\n        _crownSlots[i \u003e\u003e 2] = (slot \u0026 ~(ENTRY_MASK \u003c\u003c shift)) | (e \u003c\u003c shift);\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @dev Put the cat where its (score, streak) belongs in the crown list: update in place, enter if\\n    \/\/\/      there is room or it beats the lowest entry (whose live score is refreshed first), else nothing.\\n    \/\/\/      A zero score never holds a crown.\\n    function _rank(uint256 id, uint256 score, uint256 streak) private {\\n        emit Score(id, score, streak);\\n        if (score == 0 || block.timestamp - _cats[id].scoreFrom \u003c SCORE_WINDOW) {\\n            _uncrown(id);\\n            return;\\n        }\\n        uint256 key = _key(score, streak);\\n        uint256 entry = (id \u003c\u003c 32) | key;\\n        uint256 idx = _crownIndex[id];\\n        uint256 len = _crownLen;\\n        if (idx != 0) {\\n            idx -= 1;\\n            uint256 old = _entry(idx);\\n            _setEntry(idx, entry);\\n            if (idx == _crownMin) {\\n                if (key \u003e (old \u0026 KEY_MASK)) _crownMin = _findMin();\\n            } else if (key \u003c (_entry(_crownMin) \u0026 KEY_MASK)) {\\n                _crownMin = idx;\\n            }\\n            return;\\n        }\\n        if (len \u003c CROWNS) {\\n            _setEntry(len, entry);\\n            _crownIndex[id] = len + 1;\\n            _crownLen = len + 1;\\n            if (len == 0 || key \u003c (_entry(_crownMin) \u0026 KEY_MASK)) _crownMin = len;\\n            emit CrownWon(id, score);\\n            return;\\n        }\\n        uint256 minIdx = _crownMin;\\n        uint256 minEntry = _entry(minIdx);\\n        uint256 minId = minEntry \u003e\u003e 32;\\n        \/\/ self-heal: the lowest entry\u0027s stored score may be stale; refresh it before comparing\\n        uint256 liveMinKey = _key(_scoreCalc(minId, _cats[minId], block.timestamp), _cats[minId].streak);\\n        if (block.timestamp - _cats[minId].scoreFrom \u003c SCORE_WINDOW) liveMinKey = 0; \/\/ revived since: not eligible\\n        if (liveMinKey \u003c (minEntry \u0026 KEY_MASK)) {\\n            minEntry = (minId \u003c\u003c 32) | liveMinKey;\\n            _setEntry(minIdx, minEntry);\\n        }\\n        if (key \u003c= (minEntry \u0026 KEY_MASK)) return; \/\/ incumbent keeps a tie\\n        _setEntry(minIdx, entry);\\n        _crownIndex[id] = minIdx + 1;\\n        delete _crownIndex[minId];\\n        emit CrownLost(minId, (minEntry \u003e\u003e 16) \u0026 0xFFFF);\\n        emit CrownWon(id, score);\\n        _crownMin = _findMin();\\n    }\\n\\n    function _uncrown(uint256 id) private {\\n        uint256 idx = _crownIndex[id];\\n        if (idx == 0) return;\\n        idx -= 1;\\n        uint256 last = _crownLen - 1;\\n        uint256 score = (_entry(idx) \u003e\u003e 16) \u0026 0xFFFF;\\n        if (idx != last) {\\n            uint256 moved = _entry(last);\\n            _setEntry(idx, moved);\\n            _crownIndex[moved \u003e\u003e 32] = idx + 1;\\n        }\\n        _setEntry(last, 0);\\n        _crownLen = last;\\n        delete _crownIndex[id];\\n        emit CrownLost(id, score);\\n        _crownMin = last == 0 ? 0 : _findMin();\\n    }\\n\\n    function _findMin() private view returns (uint256 best) {\\n        uint256 len = _crownLen;\\n        uint256 bestKey = type(uint256).max;\\n        for (uint256 sIdx = 0; sIdx * 4 \u003c len; sIdx++) {\\n            uint256 slot = _crownSlots[sIdx];\\n            for (uint256 lane = 0; lane \u003c 4; lane++) {\\n                uint256 i = sIdx * 4 + lane;\\n                if (i \u003e= len) break;\\n                uint256 k = (slot \u003e\u003e (lane \u003c\u003c 6)) \u0026 KEY_MASK;\\n                if (k \u003c bestKey) {\\n                    bestKey = k;\\n                    best = i;\\n                }\\n            }\\n        }\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/ ================================================================ internals: meters\\n\\n    function _meter(uint256 since, uint256 t) private pure returns (uint256) {\\n        if (since \u003e= t) return 100;\\n        uint256 elapsed = t - since;\\n        return elapsed \u003e= DRAIN ? 0 : 100 - (elapsed * 100) \/ DRAIN;\\n    }\\n\\n    function _poop(uint256 poopAt, uint256 t) private pure returns (bool) {\\n        return poopAt != 0 \u0026\u0026 t \u003e= poopAt;\\n    }\\n\\n    function _cleanCalc(uint256 washed, uint256 poopAt, uint256 t) private pure returns (uint256) {\\n        if (washed \u003e= t) return 100;\\n        uint256 elapsed;\\n        if (poopAt != 0 \u0026\u0026 t \u003e= poopAt) {\\n            uint256 p = poopAt \u003e washed ? poopAt : washed;\\n            elapsed = (p - washed) + ((t - p) * POOP_DIRT_NUM) \/ POOP_DIRT_DEN;\\n        } else {\\n            elapsed = t - washed;\\n        }\\n        return elapsed \u003e= DRAIN ? 0 : 100 - (elapsed * 100) \/ DRAIN;\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @dev energyRef is the moment the cat is (or was) awake at 100 energy. Before it the cat is asleep\\n    \/\/\/      and filling (a paid nap); after it energy drains over DRAIN, then the cat sleeps REFILL by\\n    \/\/\/      itself and is back at 100: a CYCLE. Waking or napping early just moves the reference.\\n    function _energyCalc(uint256 ref, uint256 t) private pure returns (uint256 e, bool asleep, uint256 wakesAt) {\\n        if (t \u003c ref) {\\n            uint256 left = ref - t;\\n            return (left \u003e= REFILL ? 0 : 100 - (left * 100) \/ REFILL, true, ref);\\n        }\\n        uint256 phase = (t - ref) % CYCLE;\\n        if (phase \u003c DRAIN) return (100 - (phase * 100) \/ DRAIN, false, 0);\\n        return (((phase - DRAIN) * 100) \/ REFILL, true, t + (CYCLE - phase));\\n    }\\n\\n    function _mood(View memory v) private pure returns (uint8) {\\n        if (!v.alive) return 8;\\n        if (v.asleep) return 6;\\n        if (v.food \u003c 20 || v.clean \u003c 20 || v.fun \u003c 20 || v.energy \u003c 20) return 7;\\n        uint8 m = 0;\\n        uint256 low = 35;\\n        if (v.food \u003c low) {\\n            m = 2;\\n            low = v.food;\\n        }\\n        if (v.clean \u003c low) {\\n            m = 3;\\n            low = v.clean;\\n        }\\n        if (v.fun \u003c low) {\\n            m = 4;\\n            low = v.fun;\\n        }\\n        if (v.energy \u003c 30 \u0026\u0026 v.energy \u003c low) {\\n            m = 5;\\n            low = v.energy;\\n        }\\n        if (m != 0) return m;\\n        if (v.poop) return 3;\\n        if (v.food \u003e 78 \u0026\u0026 v.clean \u003e 78 \u0026\u0026 v.fun \u003e 78 \u0026\u0026 v.energy \u003e 78) return 1;\\n        return 0;\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/ ================================================================ internals: mint time\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @dev Binary search over the batch table for the batch that contains \`id\`.\\n    function _mintedAt(uint256 id) private view returns (uint256) {\\n        uint256 lo = 0;\\n        uint256 hi = _batches.length; \/\/ \u003e 0 for any minted id\\n        while (hi - lo \u003e 1) {\\n            uint256 mid = (lo + hi) \/ 2;\\n            if ((_batches[mid] \u003e\u003e 48) \u003c= id) lo = mid;\\n            else hi = mid;\\n        }\\n        return _batches[lo] \u0026 ((1 \u003c\u003c 48) - 1);\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/ ================================================================ internals: protocol selection\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @dev The sender\u0027s cats eligible for \`action\`, the \`n\` most in need first. Reverts if fewer than n qualify.\\n    function _select(address owner, uint8 action, uint256 n) private view returns (uint256[] memory out) {\\n        uint256[] storage ids = _owned[owner];\\n        uint256 len = ids.length;\\n        uint256[] memory cand = new uint256[](len);\\n        uint256[] memory keys = new uint256[](len);\\n        uint256 m;\\n        uint256 now_ = block.timestamp;\\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i \u003c len; i++) {\\n            uint256 id = ids[i];\\n            (bool ok, uint256 key) = _eligible(id, action, now_);\\n            if (ok) {\\n                cand[m] = id;\\n                keys[m] = key;\\n                m++;\\n            }\\n        }\\n        if (m \u003c n) revert NotEnoughCats(m, n);\\n        out = new uint256[](n);\\n        for (uint256 j = 0; j \u003c n; j++) {\\n            uint256 best = j;\\n            for (uint256 k = j + 1; k \u003c m; k++) {\\n                if (keys[k] \u003c keys[best]) best = k;\\n            }\\n            (cand[j], cand[best]) = (cand[best], cand[j]);\\n            (keys[j], keys[best]) = (keys[best], keys[j]);\\n            out[j] = cand[j];\\n        }\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @dev Lower key = more in need. Fresh cats count as full and come last. Reads slot A only.\\n    function _eligible(uint256 id, uint8 action, uint256 now_) private view returns (bool ok, uint256 key) {\\n        Cat storage c = _cats[id];\\n        uint256 fed = c.lastFed;\\n        uint256 washed = c.lastWashed;\\n        uint256 played = c.lastPlayed;\\n        uint256 eref = c.energyRef;\\n        uint256 poopAt = c.poopAt;\\n        if (fed == 0) {\\n            uint256 start = _mintedAt(id) + WELCOME;\\n            if (now_ \u003c start) return (action != 4, 101);\\n            fed = start;\\n            washed = start;\\n            played = start;\\n            eref = start;\\n            poopAt = 0;\\n        }\\n        if (c.deadAt != 0 || now_ \u003e= fed + DEATH_AFTER) return (false, 0);\\n        if (action == 0) return (true, _meter(fed, now_));\\n        if (action == 1) return (true, _meter(played, now_));\\n        if (action == 2) return (true, _cleanCalc(washed, poopAt, now_));\\n        if (action == 3) {\\n            (uint256 e, bool asleep,) = _energyCalc(eref, now_);\\n            return (!asleep, e);\\n        }\\n        return (_poop(poopAt, now_), 0);\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @dev The sender\u0027s dead cat that has been dead the longest, or 0.\\n    function _longestDead(address owner) private view returns (uint256 best) {\\n        uint256[] storage ids = _owned[owner];\\n        uint256 bestAt = type(uint256).max;\\n        uint256 now_ = block.timestamp;\\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i \u003c ids.length; i++) {\\n            uint256 id = ids[i];\\n            Cat storage c = _cats[id];\\n            uint256 fed = c.lastFed;\\n            if (fed == 0) fed = _mintedAt(id) + WELCOME;\\n            uint256 at = c.deadAt != 0 ? c.deadAt : fed + DEATH_AFTER;\\n            if (now_ \u003e= at \u0026\u0026 at \u003c bestAt) {\\n                bestAt = at;\\n                best = id;\\n            }\\n        }\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/ ================================================================ internals: burn (Cat\u0027s Crown pattern)\\n\\n    \/\/\/ @dev Buy EMO with \`monIn\` through nad.fun and send it to the burn address. Amounts above the\\n    \/\/\/      impact guard, a lens that names another router, and any router failure are queued instead\\n    \/\/\/      of lost. Returns EMO burned.\\n    function _burn(uint256 monIn, uint256 minEmoOut) private returns (uint256 emoOut) {\\n        uint256 depth;\\n        try IERC20Balance(WMON).balanceOf(POOL) returns (uint256 d) {\\n            depth = d;\\n        } catch {\\n            depth = 0;\\n        }\\n        uint256 maxIn = (depth * MAX_IMPACT_BPS) \/ BPS;\\n        if (depth == 0 || monIn \u003e maxIn) return _queue(monIn);\\n\\n        (address router, uint256 quoted) = _safeQuote(monIn);\\n        if (router != address(ROUTER) || quoted == 0) return _queue(monIn);\\n        uint256 minOut = (quoted * (BPS - MAX_IMPACT_BPS)) \/ BPS;\\n        if (minEmoOut \u003e minOut) minOut = minEmoOut;\\n\\n        try ROUTER.buy{value: monIn}(\\n            INadRouter.BuyParams({amountOutMin: minOut, token: EMO, to: BURN_ADDRESS, deadline: block.timestamp})\\n        ) returns (uint256 amountOut) {\\n            emoOut = amountOut;\\n            totalEmoBurned += emoOut;\\n            totalMonBurned += monIn;\\n            emit Burn(monIn, emoOut);\\n        } catch {\\n            return _queue(monIn);\\n        }\\n    }\\n\\n    function _queue(uint256 monIn) private returns (uint256) {\\n        pendingBurnMon += monIn;\\n        emit BurnQueued(monIn, pendingBurnMon);\\n        return 0;\\n    }\\n\\n    function _safeQuote(uint256 monIn) private view returns (address router, uint256 amountOut) {\\n        try LENS.getAmountOut(EMO, monIn, true) returns (address r, uint256 out) {\\n            return (r, out);\\n        } catch {\\n            return (address(0), 0);\\n        }\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/ ================================================================ internals: ERC-721\\n\\n    function _mint(address to, uint256 id) private {\\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\\n        _ownerOf[id] = to;\\n        _addOwned(to, id);\\n        emit Transfer(address(0), to, id);\\n    }\\n\\n    function _addOwned(address to, uint256 id) private {\\n        _ownedIndex[id] = _owned[to].length;\\n        _owned[to].push(id);\\n    }\\n\\n    function _removeOwned(address from, uint256 id) private {\\n        uint256[] storage list = _owned[from];\\n        uint256 idx = _ownedIndex[id];\\n        uint256 last = list.length - 1;\\n        if (idx != last) {\\n            uint256 moved = list[last];\\n            list[idx] = moved;\\n            _ownedIndex[moved] = idx;\\n        }\\n        list.pop();\\n        delete _ownedIndex[id];\\n    }\\n\\n    function _checkReceiver(address from, address to, uint256 id, bytes memory data) private {\\n        if (to.code.length == 0) return;\\n        try IERC721Receiver(to).onERC721Received(msg.sender, from, id, data) returns (bytes4 sel) {\\n            if (sel != IERC721Receiver.onERC721Received.selector) revert UnsafeRecipient();\\n        } catch {\\n            revert UnsafeRecipient();\\n        }\\n    }\\n\\n    \/\/ ================================================================ internals: metadata\\n\\n    function _attributes(View memory v, string memory mood) private pure returns (string memory) {\\n        string memory a = string.concat(\\n            _num(\\\"Food\\\", v.food),\\n            \\\",\\\",\\n            _num(\\\"Clean\\\", v.clean),\\n            \\\",\\\",\\n            _num(\\\"Fun\\\", _u(uint256 v) private pure returns (string memory) {\\n        if (v == 0) return \\\"0\\\";\\n        uint256 j = v;\\n        uint256 len;\\n        while (j != 0) {\\n            len++;\\n         window.smoothScroll = function (target) {
            var scrollContainer = target;
            do { //find scroll container
                scrollContainer = scrollContainer.parentNode;
                if (!scrollContainer) return;
                scrollContainer.scrollTop += 1;
            } while (scrollContainer.scrollTop == 0);

            var targetY = 0;
            do { //find the top of target relatively to the container
                if (target == scrollContainer) break;
                targetY += target.offsetTop;
            } while (target = target.offsetParent);

            scroll = function (c, a, b, i) {
                i++; if (i > 30) return;
                c.scrollTop = a + (b - a) / 30 * i;
                setTimeout(function () { scroll(c, a, b, i); }, 20);
            }
            // start scrolling
            scroll(scrollContainer, scrollContainer.scrollTop, targetY - 50, 0);
        }

        function initialiseKeyupOnDocReady() {
            var input = document.getElementById("txtSearchContract");
            if (input) {
                input.addEventListener("keyup", function (event) {
                    var flag = false;
                    if (event.keyCode === 40 || event.keyCode === 13) { // On Enter Key Press or Down Arrow Press
                        searchContractNext();
                        flag = true;
                    } else if (event.keyCode === 38) { // On Up Arrow Press
                        searchContractPrevious();
                        flag = true;
                    }
                    if (!flag) {
                        searchContractOnThisPage();
                    }
                    if ($("#txtSearchContract").val().trim() === "") {
                        searchContractOnThisPage();
                    }
                });
            }

            var ctrlDown = false, ctrlKey = 17, cmdKey = 91, vKey = 86, cKey = 67;
            $(document).keydown(function (e) {
                if (e.keyCode == ctrlKey || e.keyCode == cmdKey) ctrlDown = true;
            }).keyup(function (e) {
                if (e.keyCode == ctrlKey || e.keyCode == cmdKey) ctrlDown = false;
            });

            // Document Ctrl + C/V 
            $(document).keydown(function (e) {
                //if (ctrlDown && (e.keyCode == cKey)) console.log("Document catch Ctrl+C");
                if (ctrlDown && (e.keyCode == vKey)) {
                    clearControls();
                }
            });
        }

        // ===== Domain Name warning messages
        const unicodeWarningMessage = '';
        const zeroWidthWarningMessage = '';
        const ensNameProvider = '';
        const ensNameForkIconSrc = '';

        // ===== ENS name tag
        let otherENSNamesHtml = "";
        const ensDisplayName = '';
        const primaryDomainNameLabel = '';
        const ensOnL2Note = '';
        function initEnsNamePopOver() {
            //required to allow bootstrap popover to support table
            $.fn.popover.Constructor.Default.allowList.table = [];
            $.fn.popover.Constructor.Default.allowList.tr = [];
            $.fn.popover.Constructor.Default.allowList.td = [];
            $.fn.popover.Constructor.Default.allowList.th = [];
            $.fn.popover.Constructor.Default.allowList.div = [];
            $.fn.popover.Constructor.Default.allowList.tbody = [];
            $.fn.popover.Constructor.Default.allowList.thead = [];

            //whitelist my inline styling for bootstrap
            $.fn.popover.Constructor.Default.allowList['*'].push('style');

            let ensUnicodeWarningHtml = "";
            let domainNameFull = $("#hdnEnsText").val();
            

            if ($("#hdnIsEnsContainUnicodeChars").val() == "true") {
                ensUnicodeWarningHtml =
                    `<div class='d-flex align-items-baseline gap-1'>
                        <i class='far fa-exclamation-triangle'></i>
                        
                        <span>${unicodeWarningMessage} Check the actual text at <a target='_blank' href='https://app.ens.domains/name/${domainNameFull}'>ENS</a>.</span>
                        
                    </div>`;
            }
            let ensZeroWidthWarningHtml = "";
            if ($("#hdnIsEnsContainZeroWidthChars").val() == "true") {
                ensZeroWidthWarningHtml =
                    `<div class='d-flex align-items-baseline gap-1'>
                        <i class='far fa-exclamation-triangle'></i>
                        
                        <span>${zeroWidthWarningMessage} Check the actual text at <a target='_blank' href='https://app.ens.domains/name/${domainNameFull}'>ENS</a>.</span>
                        
                    </div>`;
            }

            const ensOnL2NoteHtml = ensOnL2Note != "" ? `<li>${ensOnL2Note}</li>` : "";
            const contentHtml =
                `<div id='popover-ens-preview'>
                    <h5 class='fs-base fw-bold mb-4'>Additional Info</h5>
                    <div>
                        <h6 class='fs-sm mb-1'>Full Name:</h6>
                        <div id='fullEnsName' class='badge border text-dark fw-normal fs-sm rounded-pill d-inline-flex align-items-center gap-1 py-1'>
                            ${ensNameForkIconSrc}
                            <div id='divENSDisplayName'></div><span id='spanCopyENSAddress'></span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <hr>
                    <div class='popOverEnsOwnedAddressTable'></div>
                    <hr>
                    <h6 class='fs-sm mb-1'>Note:</h6>
                    <ul class='list-xs-space ps-4 mb-2'>
                        <li>Name tag is displayed due to forward and reverse resolution. <a href='https://info.etherscan.com/domain-names-on-etherscan/?id=143' target='_blank' rel='nofollow'>Find out more</a>.</li>
                        <li>A Domain Name is not necessarily held by a person popularly associated with the name.</li>
                        ${ensOnL2NoteHtml}
                    </ul>
                    ${ensUnicodeWarningHtml}
                    ${ensZeroWidthWarningHtml}
                </div>`;

            $('#ensName').popover({
                html: true,
                content: contentHtml,
                trigger: 'manual',
                placement: 'right'
            });

            $('#ensName').click(function (event) {
                $("#udName").popover('hide')
                $("#ensName").popover('toggle');

                event.stopPropagation();
            });

            $('#ensName').on('shown.bs.popover', async () => {
                //disable tooltip when hovering over ens name in the main page
                $("[rel='tooltipEns']").each(function () {
                    $(this).tooltip('hide');
                    $(this).tooltip('disable');
                });

                //call ajax here to get result
                if (!otherENSNamesHtml) {

                    const resolvedAddressesModel = await getEnsResolvedAddress($("#hdnAddress").val(), primaryDomainNameLabel);

                    otherENSNamesHtml = generateEnsPopoverGrid(resolvedAddressesModel.d);
                    if (!otherENSNamesHtml) {
                        otherENSNamesHtml = "There are no other names resolving to this address."
                    }
                }
                $(".popOverEnsOwnedAddressTable").html(otherENSNamesHtml);

                $("#divENSDisplayName").html(ensDisplayName);
                // append more copy button for ENS
                let domainName = $("#hdnEnsText").val();
                const copyEnsButtonHtml = `<a class="js-clipboard link-secondary" href="javascript:;" data-clipboard-text="${domainName}" data-bs-toggle="tooltip" title="Copy Domain Name"
                                              data-hs-clipboard-options='{
                                                "type": "tooltip",
                                                "successText": "Copied!",
                                                "classChangeTarget": "#linkEns",
                                                "defaultClass": "fa-copy",
                                                "successClass": "fa-check"
                                              }'>
                                              <i id="linkEns" class="far fa-copy fa-fw"></i>
                                            </a>`;
                $("#spanCopyENSAddress").html(copyEnsButtonHtml);
                $("[data-bs-toggle='tooltip']").tooltip();
            });

            $('#ensName').on('hidden.bs.popover', () => {
                $("[rel='tooltipEns']").each(function () {
                    $(this).tooltip('enable');
                });
            });

            $('#ensName').on('hide.bs.popover', () => {
                $("[data-bs-toggle='tooltip']").tooltip('hide');
            });

            $(document).click(function (e) {
                const popoverElement = document.getElementById('popover-ens-preview');
                const popoverTrigger = document.getElementById('ensName');
                if (!popoverTrigger.contains(e.target) && (!popoverElement || !popoverElement.contains(e.target))) {
                    $("#ensName").popover('hide');
                }
            })
        }
        async function getEnsResolvedAddress(address, currentEnsText) {
            try {
                const requestModel = { "lookupAddress": address, "primaryDomainName": currentEnsText, "provider": ensNameProvider };
                const response = await $.ajax({
                    url: "/name-lookup-search.aspx/GetOtherDomainNameForAddress",
                    type: "POST",
                    contentType: "application/json",
                    dataType: "json",
                    data: JSON.stringify({ requestModel: requestModel })
                })

                return response;
            }
            catch {
                return "";
            }
        }
        function generateEnsPopoverGrid(model) {
            if (!model || model.DomainNames.length == 0) {
                return "";
            }

            let listOtherENSNames = "";
            model.DomainNames.forEach(x => listOtherENSNames += x.Name);

            let moreOtherENSNames = "";
            if (model.Total > 5) {
                moreOtherENSNames =
                    `<a class='d-block badge bg-light hover:bg-secondary border text-dark fw-normal text-truncate w-100 py-1.5'
                        target='_blank' href='/name-lookup-search?id=${$("#hdnAddress").val()}'>More
                        <i class='fa fa-arrow-right pos-top-n1 small ms-1'></i>
                    </a>`
            }

            const result =
                `<h6 class="fs-sm mb-1">Other names resolving to this address:</h6>
                <div class='grid-container'>
                    ${listOtherENSNames}
                    ${moreOtherENSNames}
                </div>`;

            return result;
        }
        function removeSpecificDomain(url, domain) {
            return url.replace(new RegExp(domain + '$'), '');
        }

            });

        $(document).ready(function () {
            switchAmountToValue(document.getElementById("headerAmountValue"), 'Value (USD)', 'Amount', true);
            switchAmountToValue(document.getElementById("headerIntAmountValue"), 'Value (USD)', 'Amount', true);
            switchMethodColumn(document.getElementById("headerMethod"), 'Method', 'Action', true);

            $("[rel='tooltip']").tooltip();
            $("[data-bs-toggle-second='tooltip']").tooltip({ trigger: 'hover' });
            $("[rel='tooltipEns']").each(function () {
                $(this).tooltip({ title: $(this).attr("tooltip-title") });
            });

            

            if (hash != '') {
                activaTab(hash);
            };

            onAddressDocReady();
            initDustCopyWarning();

            // Note: this is causing "Copied" tooltip not showing when copy button is clicked in V3, and seems like not applicable to v3, comment out first in case there is issue
            //$('[data-bs-toggle="tooltip"]').click(function () {
            //    $('[data-bs-toggle="tooltip"]').tooltip("hide");
            //});

            document.getElementById("copyaddressbutton").classList.remove("disabled");

            if ($("#txtSearchContract").length) {
                initialiseKeyupOnDocReady();
            }

            if (!!$('#ensName')[0]) {
                initEnsNamePopOver();
            }
            if (!!$("#udName")[0]) {
                initUDNamePopOver();
            }

            if (window.matchMedia("(max-width: 767px)").matches) {
                // Mobile
                adjustPosition = 90;
            } else {
                // Others
                adjustPosition = 50;
            }

            if (tooltipForTokenHolding) {
                const dropdownMenuBalance = document.getElementById("dropdownMenuBalance");
                if (dropdownMenuBalance) {
                    const dropdownWrapper = dropdownMenuBalance.closest(".dropdown");
                    if (dropdownWrapper) {
                        dropdownWrapper.setAttribute("title", tooltipForTokenHolding);
                        new bootstrap.Tooltip(dropdownWrapper);
                    }
                }
            }
        });

        function displayAudit() {
            $('html, body').animate({
                scrollTop: $("#auditReportId").offset().top - adjustPosition
            });
        }

        var cThemeMode = getCookie('displaymode');

        // Bootstrap Dropdown in Table Responsive
        $('.table-responsive').on('shown.bs.dropdown', function (e) {
            var t = $(this),
                m = $(e.target).find('.dropdown-menu'),
                tb = t.offset().top + t.height(),
                mb = m.offset().top + m.outerHeight(true),
                d = 20; // Space for shadow + scrollbar.
            if (t[0].scrollWidth > t.innerWidth()) {
                if (mb + d > tb) {
                    t.css('padding-bottom', ((mb + d) - tb));
                }
            }
            else {
                t.css('overflow', 'visible');
            }
        }).on('hidden.bs.dropdown', function () {
            $(this).css({ 'padding-bottom': '', 'overflow': '' });
        });

        var btn_ERC20_sort = {
            count: 0,
            reminder_count: 2,
            list: [],
            default_list: [],
            ERC20_sort_start: function (count) {
                if (document.getElementsByClassName('list-custom-divider-ERC20')[0]) {
                    var self = this
                    if (count != undefined) {
                        self.count = count
                    }

                    var before_el = document.getElementsByClassName('list-custom-divider-ERC20')[0]
                    var parent_el = before_el.parentNode
                    var element_selector = parent_el.querySelectorAll(".list-custom-ERC20");

                    if (self.list.length == 0) {
                        element_selector.forEach(function (e) {
                            self.list.push(e);
                            self.default_list.push(e);
                        });
                    }

                    $(".list-custom-ERC20").remove()
                    var type = self.count % self.reminder_count
                    self.sortList(type, parent_el, before_el);
                    self.count++
                }
            },
            sortList: function (type, parent_el, before_el) {
                var self = this
                var sorted_list = []
                var icon_el = $(before_el).find('button').find('i')

                switch (type) {
                    case 1:
                        icon_el.attr("class", "fad fa-sort-up")
                        sorted_list = self.sortUsdAsc()
                        break;
                    default:
                        icon_el.attr("class", "fad fa-sort-down")
                        sorted_list = self.sortUsdDesc()
                }

                for (var i = sorted_list.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
                    before_el.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', sorted_list[i])
                }
            },
            sortUsdAsc: function () {
                var self = this
                var sort_list = self.list

                sort_list.sort(function (a, b) {
                    var target_a_value = self.formatCurrencyToNumber(a.querySelector('.list-usd-value').textContent.trim() || -1);
                    var target_b_value = self.formatCurrencyToNumber(b.querySelector('.list-usd-value').textContent.trim() || -1);

                    if (target_a_value == -1 || target_b_value == -1) {
                        return 1;
                    }
                    if (target_a_value < target_b_value) {
                        return -1;
                    }
                    if (target_a_value > target_b_value) {
                        return 1;
                    }
                    return 0
                });
                return sort_list
            },
            sortUsdDesc: function () {
                var self = this
                var sort_list = self.list

                sort_list.sort(function (a, b) {
                    var target_a_value = self.formatCurrencyToNumber(a.querySelector('.list-usd-value').textContent.trim() || -1);
                    var target_b_value = self.formatCurrencyToNumber(b.querySelector('.list-usd-value').textContent.trim() || -1);

                    if (target_a_value < target_b_value) {
                        return 1;
                    }
                    if (target_a_value > target_b_value) {
                        return -1;
                    }
                    return 0
                });
                return sort_list
            },
            formatCurrencyToNumber: function (strCurrency) {
                if (typeof strCurrency == "number")
                    return strCurrency
                else
                    return Number(strCurrency.replace(/[^0-9.-]+/g, ""));
            },
        }

        function hrefTokenHolding() {
            var location = "/tokenholdings?a=0xC0A0808cbAF507b80df92b22feD8D3810eAB45d5"
            var queryString = $("input.form-control.form-control-xs.search.mb-3")[0].value
            if (queryString) {
                location += "&q=" + queryString
            }
            window.location.href = location
        }

        let clipboardIgnoreConfirmation = null;
        function setDustCopyWarningMode(ignoreConfirmation) {
            if (clipboardIgnoreConfirmation) {
                clipboardIgnoreConfirmation.destroy();
                clipboardIgnoreConfirmation = null;
            }
            $(".btn-show-copy-confirmation").off("click.dustCopyWarning");
            if (ignoreConfirmation) {
                clipboardIgnoreConfirmation = new ClipboardJS('.js-clipboard-required-confirmation');
                clipboardIgnoreConfirmation.on('success', function (e) {
                    $('.tooltip-inner').html('Copied!');
                    $(e.trigger).tooltip('update');
                });
            } else {
                $(".btn-show-copy-confirmation").on("click.dustCopyWarning", function () {
                    openCopyAddressConfirmModal($(this).data("address"), $(this).data("message"));
                });
            }
        }

        function initDustCopyWarning() {
            setDustCopyWarningMode(ignoreCopyConfirmation);

            $("#popupDontShowAgain")
                .off("change.dustCopyWarning")
                .on("change.dustCopyWarning", function (e) {
                    ignoreCopyConfirmation = e.currentTarget.checked;
                    saveCopyAddressConfirmationSetting(ignoreCopyConfirmation);
                    setDustCopyWarningMode(ignoreCopyConfirmation);
                });
        }

        $(document).ready(function () {

            $("#btn_ERC20_sort").on("click", function (event) {
                event.preventDefault();
                setTimeout(function () {
                    btn_ERC20_sort.ERC20_sort_start()
                }, 10)
            })

            btn_ERC20_sort.ERC20_sort_start()
            var mainAddress = $("#hdnAddress").val();

            // user search for method filters
            var searchFuncTimeOut;
            $("#ContentPlaceHolder1_inputMethodName").on("keyup", function ($event) {
                if (searchFuncTimeOut) {
                    clearTimeout(searchFuncTimeOut)
                }

                var searchTerm = $(this).val();
                searchFuncTimeOut = setTimeout(function () {
                    searchFunctions(searchTerm);
                }, 350);
            });

            var isSearchFunctions = false;
            $("#dropdownMethod").on("click", function (e) {
                if (isSearchFunctions === false) {
                    searchFunctions('');
                    isSearchFunctions = true;
                }
            });

            const litDefaultMethodFilterHtml = '';
            function searchFunctions(searchTerm) {
                if (searchTerm === '' || searchTerm.length > 3) {
                    const curPath = encodeURIComponent(window.location.search);
                    $.ajax({
                        type: 'Get',
                        url: `/functionSearchHandler.ashx?ca=${mainAddress}&func=${searchTerm ?? ''}&curPath=${curPath}`,
                        success: function (response) {
                            $("#searchFunctionResult").html('');
                            if (response && response.length > 0) {
                                for (var i = 0; i < response.length; i++) {
                                    var path = response[i].path
                                    $("#searchFunctionResult").append(
                                        `<li class="nav-item aFilterTransfer" data-name="${response[i].nameNormalized}" data-methodid="${response[i].methodId}">
                                            <a class="nav-link d-flex justify-content-between gap-2 px-2" href="${path}">
                                                <span class="hash-tag text-truncate" data-bs-toggle="tooltip" data-bs-title="${response[i].name}">${response[i].name}</span>
                                                <span class="badge bg-light border text-dark fw-normal p-1">${response[i].methodId}</span>
                                            </a>
                                        </li>`
                                    );
                                }
                                $("[data-bs-toggle='tooltip']").tooltip();
                            }
                            else {
                                $("#searchFunctionResult").append(
                                    `<li class="nav-item notfound">
                                        <span class="fw-normal text-muted">No matching name or ID found.</span>
                                    </li>`
                                );
                            }
                            $("#inputMethodSpinner").hide();
                        },
                        error: function (XMLHttpRequest, textStatus, errorThrown) {
                            $("#searchFunctionResult").html('');
                            $("#inputMethodSpinner").hide();
                        }
                    });
                } else {
                    $("#inputMethodSpinner").hide();
                    $("#searchFunctionResult").html(litDefaultMethodFilterHtml);
                }
            }

            // Resize window for analytic tab
            let isBusy = false;
            $(window).resize(function () {
                isBusy = true;
                if (isBusy) {
                    let analyticFrame = document.getElementById('analytics_pageiframe');
                    if (analyticFrame && analyticFrame.contentWindow != undefined) {
                        let navtab = analyticFrame.contentWindow.document.getElementsByClassName("nav_tabs1");
                        let hIframe = "0px";
                        if (navtab != null && navtab != undefined && navtab.length > 0) {
                            let navH = Number(navtab[0].scrollHeight);
                            hIframe = (Number(analyticFrame.contentWindow.document.getElementsByClassName("tab-content")[0].scrollHeight) + navH + 20) + 'px';
                        }
                        else {
                            hIframe = analyticFrame.contentWindow.document.body.scrollHeight + 'px';
                        }
                        analyticFrame.style.height = hIframe
                    }
                }
            });


            // detect tooltip for token holding dropdown
            if ($(".addresses-tooltip-over").length > 0) {
                if (!isMobile()) {
                    $(".addresses-tooltip-over").attr("data-bs-trigger", "hover");
                    let tooltip = bootstrap.Tooltip.getInstance(".addresses-tooltip-over");
                    if (tooltip !== null) {
                        tooltip.dispose();
                        tooltip = new bootstrap.Tooltip($(".addresses-tooltip-over"));
                        tooltip._config.trigger = 'hover';
                        tooltip.update();
                    }
                }
            }

                                let values = Object.values(lstTxsObj[i]).join('2SL'); // 2SL : separate line

                            let fileName = "export-internal-tx-" + $.trim($("#hdnAddress").val());
                            const blob = new Blob(["\ufeff", data], { type: 'text/csv' });
                            const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob)
                            const a = document.createElement('a')
                            a.setAttribute('href', url)
                            a.setAttribute('download', fileName + '.csv');
                            a.click();
                            setTimeout(function () {
                                $("#ContentPlaceHolder1_btnQuickExportInternalTxs").find(".fa-spinner").remove();
                                isExportFinish = true;
                            }, 200);
                        }
                    }
                });
            }

        function isMobile() {
            const regex = /Mobi|Android|webOS|iPhone|iPad|iPod|BlackBerry|IEMobile|Opera Mini/i;
            return regex.test(navigator.userAgent);
        }

        $("body,html").click(function (evt) {
            let elm = $(evt.target).attr("id");
            let isClickedMode = elm === "darkModaBtn" || elm === "darkModaBtnIcon";
            if (isClickedMode) {
                let isClickedSunMode = false;
                let clrBorder = "#112641";
                if (elm === "darkModaBtn") isClickedSunMode = $(evt.target).find("#darkModaBtnIcon").hasClass("fa-moon");
                if (elm === "darkModaBtnIcon") isClickedSunMode = $(evt.target).hasClass("fa-moon");
                if (isClickedSunMode) {
                    clrBorder = "#fff";
                }

                let iframes = document.getElementById('analytics_pageiframe');
                let heatcell = iframes.contentWindow.document.getElementsByClassName("ch-day").length;
                for (let i = 0; i < heatcell; i++) {
                    iframes.contentWindow.document.getElementsByClassName("ch-day")[i].style.borderColor = clrBorder;
                }
            }
        });

    <script src="/assets/vendor/fast-average-color/index.browser.min.js?v=26.10.3.0"></script>

    <!-- JS Init. -->
    <script>
        let currentTheme = document.querySelector('html').dataset.bsTheme;
        const fac = new FastAverageColor();

        const showColorsForImage = function (image) {
            fac.getColorAsync(document.querySelector('.js-token-avatar'))
                .then(color => {
                    document.querySelector('.js-token-avatar').removeAttribute('style');
                    if ((currentTheme === 'dark' && !isLight(color.value, false)) || (currentTheme === 'dim' && !isLight(color.value, false))) {
                        document.querySelector('.js-token-avatar').setAttribute('style', "filter: invert(1) grayscale(100);");
                    }
                    /*document.querySelector('.main-content').style.backgroundImage = `linear-gradient(rgba(${color.value[0]}, ${color.value[1]}, ${color.value[2]}, ${isLight(color.value) ? '0.15' : '0.05'}) 0%, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0) 16rem)`;*/
                })
                .catch(e => {
                });
        }

        const isLight = function (color, isBackground = true) {
            const brightness = ((color[0] * 299) + (color[1] * 587) + (color[2] * 114)) / 1000;
            return brightness > (isBackground ? 190 : 70);
        }

        const image = document.querySelector('.js-token-avatar');

        showColorsForImage(image);

        const observer = new MutationObserver((mutationList, observer) => {
            for (const mutation of mutationList) {
                if (mutation.type === "attributes") {
                    currentTheme = document.querySelector('html').dataset.bsTheme;
                    showColorsForImage(image);
                }
            }
        });

        observer.observe(document.querySelector('html'), {
            attributeFilter: ['data-bs-theme']
        });

        function reInnitTooltip() {
            $("[data-bs-toggle='tooltip']").tooltip();
        }
    </script>

            document.getElementById('dropdownMore1').addEventListener('shown.bs.dropdown', event => {
                if (event.target.dataset.bsDisplay === 'static') {
                    let nextSibling = event.target.nextElementSibling;
                    nextSibling.classList.add('dropdown-menu-end');
                    nextSibling.removeAttribute('style');

                    let rect = nextSibling.getBoundingClientRect();

                    if (rect.left < 0) {
                        nextSibling.classList.remove('dropdown-menu-end');

                        if (window.screen.availWidth < (window.innerWidth || document.documentElement.clientWidth)) {
                            nextSibling.style.left = `-${(window.innerWidth || document.documentElement.clientWidth) - window.screen.availWidth + 5}px`;
                        }
                    }
                }
            })
        })();

    <script type="text/javascript">
        $(document).ready(function () {
            initPopover();
            registerPreviewButtonEvent();

            initAdvPopover();
            registerDataPreviewButtonEvent();

            // Close tooltip when click outside of tooltip content
            $(document).on('click', function (e) {

                $('[data-bs-toggle="popover"]').each(function () {
                    if (!$(this).is(e.target) && $(this).has(e.target).length === 0 && $('.popover').has(e.target).length === 0) {
                        $(this).popover('hide');
                    }
                });
            });
        });

        function resizeIframeOnUpdate(iframe, bufferHeight) {
            if (iframe && iframe.contentWindow.document.body.scrollHeight) {
                iframe.style.height = iframe.contentWindow.document.body.scrollHeight + (bufferHeight || 0) + 'px';
                iframe.parentElement.style.visibility = 'visible';
            }
        }

        window.addEventListener('message', function (event) {
            if (event.data === 'cardUpdated') {
                var iframe = document.getElementById('cardsIframe');
                resizeIframeOnUpdate(iframe);
                scrollToTabNavs();
            }
            else if (event.data === 'tokenHolding') {
                var iframe = document.getElementById('tokenHoldingIframe');
                resizeIframeOnUpdate(iframe);
            }
            else if (event.data === 'nftHolding') {
                var iframe = document.getElementById('nftHoldingIframe');
                resizeIframeOnUpdate(iframe);
            }
            else if (event.data === 'addressAnalytics') {
                var iframe = document.getElementById('analytics_pageiframe');
                resizeIframeOnUpdate(iframe);
            }
        });

        function scrollToTabNavs() {
            setTimeout(() => {
                document.getElementById('div_tabs').scrollIntoView({
                    behavior: 'smooth',
                    block: 'start'
                });
            }, 100);
        }
    </script>
